# topic-scenario
Archive source: drafts
Audit summary: average overall 3.51 across 112 items.
## Rubric
- `source.shortMeaning`: 8-18 words. Do not restate the cited BaniDB translation verbatim or as a token-rewrite. Name the turn the line makes.
- `summary`: 1-2 sentences. Keep each sentence at 24 words or fewer. Name the emotional state the line meets rather than repeating the topic label.
- `takeaway`: one sentence, 14 words or fewer. Make it concrete and end on a clear verb or precise noun.
- `lifeApplication`: 1-2 sentences. Name a specific time, place, or body cue such as the mouth, the doorway, before the reply, or before the message is sent. Do not use template phrasing.
- `whyItMatters`: say who the item is for and what changes when they hold it.
- `scenario excerpt`: quote or paraphrase the cited line directly enough that the source is still visible.
- `scenario practicalGuidance`: give one concrete move the reader can take next.
- `collection description`: stay under 40 words, name the through-line, and avoid `living archive`, `composed ritual`, or similar shell language.
- Avoid every hard-banned phrase from the style guide, especially the recycled template lines that were removed from the pipeline.
- Human approval is required before publish. Keep edits faithful to the cited verses, emotionally honest, and specific enough to survive ordinary use.
Edit the JSON-compatible YAML blocks in place. Apply changes with `npm run learn:review:apply`.

### Cited Verses
- Source 1: `shabad-child-of-grace` · verseIds 3757, 3760
  - `3757` ਕੋਈ ਸਜਣੁ ਸੰਤੁ ਮਿਲੈ ਵਡਭਾਗੀ ਮੈ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਪਿਆਰਾ ਦਸੈ ਜੀਉ ॥੧॥
    English: If only I could have the good fortune to meet some friendly Saint; he might show me the Way to my Beloved Lord God. ||1||
  - `3760` ਮਿਲਿ ਸਤਸੰਗਤਿ ਖੋਜੁ ਦਸਾਈ ਵਿਚਿ ਸੰਗਤਿ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਵਸੈ ਜੀਉ ॥੨॥
    English: Joining the Sat Sangat, the True Congregation, I ask about the Path to God. In that Congregation, the Lord God abides. ||2||
- Source 2: `shabad-search-ends-in-saint-company` · verseIds 3898, 3899, 3900
  - `3898` ਇਕੁ ਖਿਨੁ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਸਾਂਤਿ ਨ ਆਵੈ ਜੋਗੀ ਬਹੁੜਿ ਬਹੁੜਿ ਉਠਿ ਧਾਵੈ ਜੀਉ ॥੩॥
    English: and yet, their hearts are not at peace, even for an instant. The Yogi rises up and goes out, over and over again. ||3||
  - `3899` ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਮੋਹਿ ਸਾਧੁ ਮਿਲਾਇਆ ॥
    English: By His Mercy, I have met the Holy Saint.
  - `3900` ਮਨੁ ਤਨੁ ਸੀਤਲੁ ਧੀਰਜੁ ਪਾਇਆ ॥
    English: My mind and body have been cooled and soothed; I have been blessed with patience and composure.
- Source 3: `shabad-lord-keeps-the-humble-close` · verseIds 37474, 37476
  - `37474` ਹਰਿ ਜਸੁ ਜਪਿ ਜਪੁ ਵਡਾ ਵਡੇਰਾ ਗੁਰਮੁਖਿ ਰਖਉ ਉਰਿ ਧਾਰੇ ॥
    English: One who chants the Praises of the Lord is the greatest of the great; the Gurmukh keeps the Lord clasped to his heart.
  - `37476` ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਹੈ ਹਰਿ ਰਾਖੈ ਕੰਠਿ ਜਨ ਧਾਰੇ ॥
    English: The Lord is close to His humble servant, and His humble servant is close to the Lord; He keeps His humble servant clasped to His Heart.

  - `9416` ਉਸਤਤਿ ਨਿੰਦਾ ਏ ਸਮ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਲੋਭੁ ਮੋਹੁ ਫੁਨਿ ਤੈਸਾ ॥੨॥

  - `499` ਜੋ ਤਉ ਭਾਵੈ ਸੋਈ ਥੀਸੀ ਜੋ ਤੂੰ ਦੇਹਿ ਸੋਈ ਹਉ ਪਾਈ ॥੧॥ ਰਹਾਉ ॥

  - `503` ਤੁਧੁ ਆਪਿ ਵਿਛੋੜਿਆ ਆਪਿ ਮਿਲਾਇਆ ॥੧॥

  - `37474` ਹਰਿ ਜਸੁ ਜਪਿ ਜਪੁ ਵਡਾ ਵਡੇਰਾ ਗੁਰਮੁਖਿ ਰਖਉ ਉਰਿ ਧਾਰੇ ॥
    English: One who chants the Praises of the Lord is the greatest of the great; the Gurmukh keeps the Lord clasped to his heart.
  - `37476` ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਹੈ ਹਰਿ ਰਾਖੈ ਕੰਠਿ ਜਨ ਧਾਰੇ ॥

    English: and yet, their hearts are not at peace, even for an instant. The Yogi rises up and goes out, over and over again. ||3||
  - `3899` ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਮੋਹਿ ਸਾਧੁ ਮਿਲਾਇਆ ॥

  - `3900` ਮਨੁ ਤਨੁ ਸੀਤਲੁ ਧੀਰਜੁ ਪਾਇਆ ॥
    English: My mind and body have been cooled and soothed; I have been blessed with patience and composure.
- Source 2: `shabad-lord-keeps-the-humble-close` · verseIds 37474, 37476, 37477
  - `37474` ਹਰਿ ਜਸੁ ਜਪਿ ਜਪੁ ਵਡਾ ਵਡੇਰਾ ਗੁਰਮੁਖਿ ਰਖਉ ਉਰਿ ਧਾਰੇ ॥
    English: One who chants the Praises of the Lord is the greatest of the great; the Gurmukh keeps the Lord clasped to his heart.
  - `37476` ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਹਰਿ ਜਨ ਹੈ ਹਰਿ ਰਾਖੈ ਕੰਠਿ ਜਨ ਧਾਰੇ ॥
    English: The Lord is close to His humble servant, and His humble servant is close to the Lord; He keeps His humble servant clasped to His Heart.
  - `37477` ਨਾਨਕ ਪਿਤਾ ਮਾਤਾ ਹੈ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਹਮ ਬਾਰਿਕ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਰੇ ॥੪॥੬॥੧੮॥
    English: O Nanak, the Lord God is our father and mother. I am His child; the Lord cherishes me. ||4||6||18||
- Source 3: `shabad-human-birth-is-chance` · verseIds 528, 529, 533
  - `528` ਸਰੰਜਾਮਿ ਲਾਗੁ ਭਵਜਲ ਤਰਨ ਕੈ ॥
    English: Make every effort to cross over this terrifying world-ocean.
  - `529` ਜਨਮੁ ਬ੍ਰਿਥਾ ਜਾਤ ਰੰਗਿ ਮਾਇਆ ਕੈ ॥੧॥ ਰਹਾਉ ॥
    English: You are squandering this life uselessly in the love of Maya. ||1||Pause||
  - `533` ਸਰਣਿ ਪਰੇ ਕੀ ਰਾਖਹੁ ਸਰਮਾ ॥੨॥
    English: O Lord, I seek Your Sanctuary; please, preserve my honor! ||2||
